import { v } from 'convex/values'
import { query, mutation } from './_generated/server'
import { internal } from './_generated/api'
import {
  getClerkOrganizationRole,
  requireIdentity,
  requireActiveClerkOrganization,
  requireTenantRole,
  type TenantRole,
} from './authHelpers'
import type { Id } from './_generated/dataModel'
import { ensureCaregiverEmployeeProfile } from './employeeProfiles'

export const list = query({
  args: {},
  handler: async (ctx) => {
    const identity = await requireIdentity(ctx)
    const memberships = await ctx.db
      .query('tenantMembers')
      .withIndex('by_clerk_user_id', (q) =>
        q.eq('clerkUserId', identity.subject),
      )
      .collect()

    const tenants = []
    for (const m of memberships) {
      const tenant = await ctx.db.get(m.tenantId)
      if (tenant) {
        tenants.push({ ...tenant, role: m.role })
      }
    }
    return tenants
  },
})

export const get = query({
  args: { clerkOrgId: v.string() },
  handler: async (ctx, { clerkOrgId }) => {
    const { tenant, role } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, clerkOrgId, [
      'org:admin',
      'org:coordinator',
      'org:caregiver',
    ])
    return { ...tenant, role }
  },
})

export const create = mutation({
  args: {
    clerkOrgId: v.string(),
    name: v.string(),
    slug: v.string(),
  },
  handler: async (ctx, { clerkOrgId, name, slug }) => {
    const identity = await requireIdentity(ctx)
    requireActiveClerkOrganization(identity, clerkOrgId)

    const existing = await ctx.db
      .query('tenants')
      .withIndex('by_clerk_org_id', (q) => q.eq('clerkOrgId', clerkOrgId))
      .unique()

    if (existing) {
      throw new Error('Tenant already exists for this organization.')
    }

    const tenantId = await ctx.db.insert('tenants', {
      clerkOrgId,
      name,
      slug,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    })

    await ctx.db.insert('tenantMembers', {
      tenantId,
      clerkUserId: identity.subject,
      role: getClerkOrganizationRole(identity) ?? 'org:admin',
      displayName: identity.name ?? identity.email ?? 'User',
      email: identity.email ?? '',
    })

    return tenantId
  },
})

export const ensureSelectedAgency = mutation({
  args: {
    clerkOrgId: v.string(),
    name: v.string(),
    slug: v.string(),
    displayName: v.string(),
    email: v.string(),
  },
  handler: async (ctx, { clerkOrgId, name, slug, displayName, email }) => {
    const identity = await requireIdentity(ctx)
    requireActiveClerkOrganization(identity, clerkOrgId)

    const tenant = await ctx.db
      .query('tenants')
      .withIndex('by_clerk_org_id', (q) => q.eq('clerkOrgId', clerkOrgId))
      .unique()

    let tenantId: Id<'tenants'>
    let isNewTenant = false

    if (!tenant) {
      tenantId = await ctx.db.insert('tenants', {
        clerkOrgId,
        name,
        slug,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      })
      isNewTenant = true
    } else {
      tenantId = tenant._id
    }

    const existingMember = await ctx.db
      .query('tenantMembers')
      .withIndex('by_tenant_user', (q) =>
        q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('clerkUserId', identity.subject),
      )
      .unique()

    if (existingMember) {
      await ctx.db.patch(existingMember._id, {
        displayName,
        email,
      })
      return { tenantId, memberId: existingMember._id, isNewTenant }
    }

    const roleFromClerk = getClerkOrganizationRole(identity)
    const role: TenantRole = isNewTenant
      ? 'org:admin'
      : roleFromClerk ?? 'org:caregiver'

    const memberId = await ctx.db.insert('tenantMembers', {
      tenantId,
      clerkUserId: identity.subject,
      role,
      displayName,
      email,
    })

    if (role === 'org:caregiver') {
      const newMember = await ctx.db.get(memberId)
      if (newMember) {
        const employeeProfileId = await ensureCaregiverEmployeeProfile(
          ctx,
          tenantId,
          newMember,
        )
        if (employeeProfileId) {
          await ctx.scheduler.runAfter(0, internal.adpOutbound.adpSyncWorker, {
            employeeProfileId,
          })
        }
      }
    }

    return { tenantId, memberId, isNewTenant }
  },
})

export const updateBillingSettings = mutation({
  args: {
    clerkOrgId: v.string(),
    defaultRate: v.number(),
    exportFormat: v.union(v.literal('csv'), v.literal('json')),
  },
  handler: async (ctx, args) => {
    const { tenantId } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, args.clerkOrgId, [
      'org:admin',
    ])
    await ctx.db.patch(tenantId, {
      billingSettings: {
        defaultRate: args.defaultRate,
        exportFormat: args.exportFormat,
      },
    })
  },
})

export const seedAgency = mutation({
  args: {

    }

        .collect()

    const findShift = async (scheduledStart: string) => {

    // Helper: repair missing child records for a shift
    const repairShiftChildren = async (

        tasks: Array<{ title: string; requiredProof: boolean; status: 'pending' | 'complete'; proofName?: string }>

    ) => {

            tenantId,

            requiredProof: task.requiredProof,
            status: task.status,
            proofName: task.proofName,

          repaired = true
        }

      if (config.review) {
        const existingReviews = await ctx.db
          .query('reviewEvents')
          .withIndex('by_tenant_shift', (q) => q.eq('tenantId', tenantId).eq('shiftId', shiftId))
          .collect()
        if (existingReviews.length === 0) {

    if (shift4Repaired) counts.shiftsRepaired++

    // Upsert compliance docs
    const findDoc = async (title: string) => {
      const all = await ctx.db
        .query('complianceDocs')
        .withIndex('by_tenant_category', (q) => q.eq('tenantId', tenantId))
        .collect()
      return all.find((d) => d.title === title) ?? null

    const doc1 = await findDoc('SLS Documentation Requirements')

      })
      counts.docsCreated++
    } else {
      counts.docsSkipped++
    }

    const doc2 = await findDoc('ILS Billing Guidelines')
    if (!doc2) {
      await ctx.db.insert('complianceDocs', {
        tenantId,
        title: 'ILS Billing Guidelines',
        body: 'ILS services are billed at the authorized rate per hour. Documentation must show measurable skill acquisition.',
        category: 'billing',
        visibility: 'all_staff',
        embedding: generateEmbedding('ILS Billing Guidelines authorized rate per hour skill acquisition'),
      })
      counts.docsCreated++
    } else {
      counts.docsSkipped++
    }

    const totalCreated =
      counts.clientsCreated +
      counts.shiftsCreated +
      counts.docsCreated
    const totalSkipped =
      counts.clientsSkipped +
      counts.shiftsSkipped +
      counts.docsSkipped

    if (totalCreated === 0 && totalSkipped > 0 && counts.shiftsRepaired === 0) {
      return {
        status: 'already-seeded' as const,
        message: `All demo data already exists (${totalSkipped} items skipped).`,
        counts,
      }
    }

    if (counts.shiftsRepaired > 0) {
      return {
        status: 'seeded' as const,
        message: `Repaired ${counts.shiftsRepaired} shift${counts.shiftsRepaired !== 1 ? 's' : ''} with missing records. ${totalCreated > 0 ? `Created ${totalCreated} new items.` : ''}`,
        counts,
      }
    }

    return {
      status: 'seeded' as const,
      message: `Seeded ${totalCreated} new items. ${totalSkipped > 0 ? `${totalSkipped} existing items skipped.` : ''}`,
      counts,
    }
  },
})

export const seedCaregiverShifts = mutation({
  args: {
    clerkOrgId: v.string(),
    caregiverId: v.string(),
  },
  handler: async (ctx, args) => {
    const { tenantId } = await requireTenantRole(ctx, args.clerkOrgId, [
      'org:admin',
      'org:coordinator',
    ])

    const clients = await ctx.db
      .query('clients')
      .withIndex('by_tenant', (q) => q.eq('tenantId', tenantId))
      .take(10)

    if (clients.length === 0) {
      throw new Error('No clients found. Seed agency data first.')
    }

    const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
    const findShift = async (scheduledStart: string) => {
      const all = await ctx.db
        .query('shifts')
        .withIndex('by_tenant_status_start', (q) =>
          q.eq('tenantId', tenantId),
        )
        .collect()
      return all.find((s) => s.scheduledStart === scheduledStart) ?? null
    }

    const counts = { created: 0, skipped: 0 }

    // Demo shift 1: scheduled for today morning
    const start1 = `${today}T09:00:00Z`
    if (!(await findShift(start1))) {
      const client = clients[0]
      await ctx.db.insert('shifts', {
        tenantId,
        clientId: client._id,
        caregiverId: args.caregiverId,
        scheduledStart: start1,
        scheduledEnd: `${today}T13:00:00Z`,
        status: 'scheduled',
        serviceType: client.serviceType,
        rate: 28.5,
      })
      counts.created++
    } else {
      counts.skipped++
    }

    // Demo shift 2: scheduled for today afternoon
    const start2 = `${today}T14:00:00Z`
    if (!(await findShift(start2))) {
      const client = clients.length > 1 ? clients[1] : clients[0]
      await ctx.db.insert('shifts', {
        tenantId,
        clientId: client._id,
        caregiverId: args.caregiverId,
        scheduledStart: start2,
        scheduledEnd: `${today}T18:00:00Z`,
        status: 'scheduled',
        serviceType: client.serviceType,
        rate: 30.0,
      })
      counts.created++
    } else {
      counts.skipped++
    }

    return {
      status: counts.created > 0 ? ('seeded' as const) : ('already-seeded' as const),
      message:
        counts.created > 0
          ? `Created ${counts.created} demo shift${counts.created !== 1 ? 's' : ''} for caregiver.`
          : `All demo shifts already exist (${counts.skipped} skipped).`,
      counts,
    }
  },
})

function generateEmbedding(text: string): number[] {
  const DIMENSIONS = 32
  const vector = Array.from({ length: DIMENSIONS }, () => 0)
  const tokens = text
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s-]/g, ' ')
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter(Boolean)

  tokens.forEach((token, index) => {
    const hash = hashToken(`${token}:${index}`)
    const slot = Math.abs(hash) % DIMENSIONS
    const sign = hash % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1
    vector[slot] += sign * (1 + token.length / 12)
  })

  const magnitude = Math.sqrt(
    vector.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value ** 2, 0),
  )
  return magnitude === 0
    ? vector
    : vector.map((value) => Number((value / magnitude).toFixed(6)))
}

function hashToken(token: string): number {
  let hash = 2166136261
  for (let index = 0; index < token.length; index += 1) {
    hash ^= token.charCodeAt(index)
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 16777619)
  }
  return hash
}

**Implementation Plan**

## 1. Goal And Acceptance Criteria

Implement ATRIA-X Phase 1 caregiver identity, ADP sync controls, and geofence settings with minimal backend and thin admin/coordinator UI.

Acceptance criteria:

- Admins can create a caregiver using `displayName` and `email`.
- Caregiver creation creates or updates an `employeeProfiles` row, sends the existing Clerk invitation, and schedules `adpSyncWorker`.
- If ADP is not configured, caregiver ADP sync lands in `pending_credentials`.
- Invite acceptance links the existing `employeeProfiles` row to `clerkUserId` and `tenantMemberId`.
- Admins can run an ADP initial worker load and get summary counts for `matched`, `created`, and `errors`.
- Admin-facing employee profile list returns `displayName`, `email`, `role`, and `adpSyncStatus`.
- Team UI adds a â€œCaregivers & ADP syncâ€ panel with caregiver form, employee list, status pills, and â€œLoad from ADPâ€.
- Admins/coordinators can view and edit clock-in location rules; caregivers are rejected by backend.
- Client location editing supports service address plus latitude/longitude and warns when geofence is enabled but coordinates are missing.
- Required gates pass: `npx convex codegen`, `npm run lint`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run test`.

## 2. Files To Modify Or Create

Modify:

- `convex/members.ts`
- `convex/employeeProfiles.ts`
- `convex/invitations.ts`
- `convex/adpSync.ts`
- `convex/adpOutbound.ts`
- `convex/tenantSettings.ts`
- `convex/clients.ts`
- `convex/tenants.ts`
- `convex/adpSync.test.ts`
- `convex/tenantSettings.test.ts`

## 3. Implementation Approach

1. Backend caregiver helpers
   - Extend `convex/employeeProfiles.ts` with reusable helpers:
     - normalize email consistently.
     - find profile by tenant/email.
     - upsert invited caregiver profile by email/display name.
     - link profile to accepted caregiver member.
   - Preserve existing `ensureCaregiverEmployeeProfile` behavior, but make it link by normalized email first, then name as fallback.

2. `createCaregiver`
   - Because Convex mutations cannot directly perform external `fetch`, keep external Clerk invitation behavior in an action.
   - Add an admin-callable public API named `createCaregiver`, preferably in `convex/members.ts` as an action or action-backed flow:
     - validate admin via `api.members.me` or a new internal mutation guarded by `requireTenantRole(ctx, clerkOrgId, ['org:admin'])`.
     - create/update `employeeProfiles` with role intent `org:caregiver`.
     - call existing Clerk invitation logic from `convex/invitations.ts`.
     - schedule `internal.adpOutbound.adpSyncWorker`.
   - If strict generated API naming requires â€œmutationâ€, use a public mutation for DB work plus a public action wrapper for Clerk invite, but expose the UI to the action so the invitation actually sends.

3. Invite acceptance profile linking
   - Update `convex/tenants.ts` `ensureSelectedAgency`.
   - When an existing member is patched and their role is caregiver, call the profile-link helper.
   - When a new caregiver member is inserted, link any pre-created invited profile by normalized email, patch `clerkUserId` and `tenantMemberId`, then enqueue `adpSyncWorker`.

4. Employee profile listing
   - Add `listEmployeeProfiles` in `convex/members.ts` or `convex/employeeProfiles.ts`.
   - Guard with `requireTenantRole(ctx, clerkOrgId, ['org:admin'])`.
   - Query `employeeProfiles.by_tenant`.
   - Return only `displayName`, `email`, `role`, `adpSyncStatus`, plus `_id` if needed for React keys.
   - Derive `role` from linked `tenantMemberId` when present; otherwise default invited/unlinked profiles to `org:caregiver`.

5. ADP initial load summary
   - Replace or supplement `api.adpSync.triggerInitialWorkerLoad` with `runAdpInitialWorkerLoad`.
   - Guard with `requireTenantRole(ctx, clerkOrgId, ['org:admin'])`.
   - Run the existing `internal.adpOutbound.adpInitialWorkerLoad` in a way that returns the result to the caller.
   - Extend `adpInitialWorkerLoad` result shape to always include `matched`, `created`, and `errors`.
   - On configured ADP failure, return `errors: 1` with sanitized error while still recording integration event and retry metadata.

6. Geofence settings backend
   - Keep `convex/tenantSettings.ts` as the source.
   - Confirm `updateShiftGeofence` allows only `org:admin` and `org:coordinator`.
   - Add real Convex tests proving caregiver rejection instead of only source-string tests.

7. Team UI
   - Broaden `/team` route in `src/app/router.tsx` and sidebar roles in `src/app/shell/Sidebar.tsx` to `org:admin` and `org:coordinator`.
   - Keep admin-only controls gated inside `TeamPage`.
   - Add `Caregivers & ADP sync` panel:
     - `useQuery(api.members.listEmployeeProfiles)`.
     - caregiver form calls `api.members.createCaregiver`.
     - â€œLoad from ADPâ€ calls `api.adpSync.runAdpInitialWorkerLoad`.
     - show result message/toast using existing inline success/error pattern.
   - Add status badge mapping:
     - `synced`, `matched`, `created` -> green â€œSyncedâ€.
     - `queued` -> amber â€œQueuedâ€.
     - `pending_credentials` -> gray â€œPending credentialsâ€.
     - `error` -> red â€œErrorâ€.

8. Clock-in location rules UI
   - Add `ClockInLocationRulesPanel` under `src/features/team/components`.
   - Use `api.tenantSettings.get` and `api.tenantSettings.updateShiftGeofence`.
   - Use `Checkbox`, `Input`, `Button`, and `Card`.
   - Visible to admins/coordinators via `/team` route guard.
   - Editable by admins/coordinators; backend remains authoritative.

9. Client location editing
   - Extend `src/features/clients/pages/ClientsPage.tsx`.
   - Add compact edit controls per client or a dialog matching existing scheduling dialog patterns.
   - Call `api.clients.updateServiceAddress`.
   - Include address fields plus optional latitude/longitude.
   - Query tenant settings; if geofence enabled and a client lacks both coordinates, show warning text that clock-in/out will be blocked until coordinates are completed.

## 4. Test Plan

Unit and Convex tests:

- `convex/employeeProfiles.test.ts`
  - Admin `createCaregiver` creates profile and schedules worker.
  - Existing email updates profile rather than duplicating.
  - Missing ADP config leads to `pending_credentials` after scheduled worker drain.
  - Invite acceptance through `tenants.ensureSelectedAgency` links `clerkUserId` and `tenantMemberId`.

- `convex/adpSync.test.ts`
  - `runAdpInitialWorkerLoad` returns correct `matched`, `created`, `errors` with mocked ADP.
  - Admin allowed, coordinator/caregiver rejected.
  - Existing `triggerInitialWorkerLoad` behavior either preserved or migrated cleanly.

- `convex/tenantSettings.test.ts`
  - Admin can update geofence.
  - Coordinator can update geofence.
  - Caregiver mutation call rejects.
  - Invalid radius/accuracy rejects.

- `src/features/team/pages/TeamPage.test.tsx`
  - Renders â€œCaregivers & ADP syncâ€.
  - Create caregiver form calls the new API with name/email.
  - ADP status pills render expected words and badge variants.
  - â€œLoad from ADPâ€ displays summary result.
  - Coordinator can see clock-in location rules but not admin-only caregiver creation.

- `src/features/clients/pages/ClientsPage.test.tsx`
  - Service location fields call `updateServiceAddress`.
  - Warning appears when geofence is enabled and client coordinates are missing.

E2E scenarios:

- Admin opens Team, creates caregiver, sees queued/pending/synced status.
- Admin runs ADP load and sees summary.
- Coordinator opens Team and edits clock-in location rules.
- Caregiver cannot access Team route and backend rejects settings mutation.
- Client missing coordinates shows geofence blocking warning.

Stress/automation considerations:

- ADP initial load should remain idempotent by AOID/email/name.
- Repeated caregiver creation for the same email must not create duplicate profiles.
- Scheduled worker retries must not create duplicate ADP workers.
- Keep list queries capped or indexed if employee volume grows.

## 5. Risks, Security, And Edge Cases

- Multi-tenancy: every backend read/write must use `requireTenant`, `requireTenantRole`, and `assertTenantDoc` where tenant documents are loaded by id.
- Authorization: UI route guards are not sufficient; Convex functions must enforce roles.
- PHI/HIPAA: do not expose unnecessary caregiver or client data in admin list responses. Return only fields needed by the UI.
- Clerk role mapping: Clerk only has `org:admin` and `org:member`; ATRIA-X role must remain in Convex `tenantMembers`.
- External side effects: Clerk invitation must stay in an action, or an action-backed flow, because external HTTP belongs outside Convex mutations.
- Idempotency: caregiver create and ADP load must tolerate retries, duplicate button clicks, and invitation acceptance after an ADP import.
- Geofence blocking: if geofence is enabled and coordinates are missing, backend already blocks required punch location; UI warning should make this visible before caregivers are blocked.
- ADP unavailable: sanitize errors and avoid leaking credentials or tokens.

## 6. Rollback And Verification

Rollback:

- Revert changes in the listed files.
- Regenerate Convex API after rollback with `npx convex codegen`.
- Confirm no hand edits remain in `convex/_generated`.

Verification sequence:

1. `npx convex codegen`
2. `npm run lint`
3. `npm run typecheck`
4. `npm run test`

Manual smoke checks:

- Admin Team page shows existing members, caregiver ADP panel, geofence panel.
- Coordinator Team page shows location rules but not admin-only invite/caregiver creation controls.
- Caregiver cannot reach Team and cannot mutate geofence settings.
- Client page can save coordinates and warning disappears once latitude/longitude are both present.
- ADP load result reports matched/created/errors accurately with mocked ADP.

**Implementation Plan**

## 1. Goal And Acceptance Criteria

Port ATRIA-X frontend foundations to the dark design-system tokens and polish `src/shared/ui/*` primitives against the exported Figma recipes, without changing product behavior or backend/auth logic.

Acceptance criteria:

- `src/index.css` makes dark mode the default while preserving existing `--color-atria-*` utility names.
- Legacy light values are retained in a documented light-theme block.
- Figma references are included in implementation notes/summary:
  - Foundations frame `1:2`, `C:\Users\pinol\AppData\Local\hermes\image_cache\img_b8154a1c250c.png`
  - Components frame `1:89`, `C:\Users\pinol\AppData\Local\hermes\image_cache\img_cf40f27f5d5d.png`
- Button, Card, form controls, StatusBadge, KpiCard, AppLoader, Dialog, Checkbox, Badge, Table, Separator all use the dark recipe.
- Add missing shared primitives for `FieldGroup`, `EmptyState`, `ProgressSteps`, and `Toast`.
- Existing public component APIs keep working; changes are additive where possible.
- No Convex files are edited; no `convex/_generated` edits.
- Required gates pass before claiming green: `npm run lint`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run test`, `npm run build`.

Note: `atriax/design/design-system-tokens.md` is referenced by the prompt but is not present in this checkout. Re-check before implementation; if still missing, document that the implementation used the prompt token table plus exported Figma PNGs.

## 2. Files To Modify Or Create

Modify:

- `src/index.css`
- `src/shared/ui/Button.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/Badge.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/Card.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/Input.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/Select.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/Textarea.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/StatusBadge.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/KpiCard.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/Checkbox.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/Dialog.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/Table.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/Separator.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/AppLoader.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/index.ts`

Create:

- `src/shared/ui/FieldGroup.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/EmptyState.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/ProgressSteps.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/Toast.tsx`
- `src/shared/ui/uiPrimitives.test.tsx`

Optional only if needed to adopt the new shared primitive cleanly:

- `src/features/billing/components/EmptyBillingState.tsx`

## 3. Implementation Approach

1. Update `src/index.css`.
   - Keep Tailwind token names like `bg-atria-bg`, `text-atria-ink`, `border-atria-border`.
   - Use alias variables so dark/light can swap later, for example `--color-atria-bg: var(--atria-bg)`.
   - Set dark defaults from the required brief: `#0A0E0F`, `#11171A`, `#0C1113`, `#F2F6F7`, `#16A34A`.
   - Add status tokens: success `#2FBF71`, warning `#F0B429`, danger `#F0564A`, info `#4D8DF6`, neutral `#8A99A0`.
   - Add Figma-adjacent supporting tokens: elevated/subtle surfaces, border `#2A3437`, secondary text, per-step accents, radius `8/12/16/20/pill`, control heights `40/52`, and type scale.
   - Move current light values into `[data-theme='light']` with a clear comment.

2. Polish existing primitives.
   - `Button`: add `size="lg"` as 52px, make primary/danger/secondary dark recipes, use pill radius for large primary, preserve `sm`, `md`, `icon`, `sidebar`.
   - `Card`: dark surface, border, radius 16, default content padding 24, with existing `className` overrides still winning.
   - `Input`, `Select`, `Textarea`: use 16px control text, md 40px and lg 52px recipes, dark focus ring, disabled state, additive label/helper/error support.
   - `StatusBadge`: support `success`, `warning`, `danger`, `info`, `neutral`; render visible word plus dot, never color alone; keep current `status: ShiftStatus` API.
   - `KpiCard`: dark KPI card recipe around the Figma 260x132 shape, preserving `label`, `value`, `detail`, `icon`.
   - Update `Badge`, `Checkbox`, `Dialog`, `Table`, `Separator`, and `AppLoader` to use the same dark tokens.

3. Add missing primitives.
   - `FieldGroup`: shared label/helper/error wrapper with `htmlFor`, `aria-describedby`, `aria-invalid` support.
   - `EmptyState`: icon/title/detail/actions presentational primitive.
   - `ProgressSteps`: accessible ordered step chips with per-step accent support.
   - `Toast`: presentational alert/toast row with tone, title, description, optional action/dismiss; no global toast manager.

4. Export everything from `src/shared/ui/index.ts`.

5. Keep scope presentation-only.
   - Do not alter route guards, data fetching, Convex calls, billing rules, or tenant logic.
   - Do not reset/stash/revert existing dirty worktree changes.

## 4. Test Plan

Unit tests in `src/shared/ui/uiPrimitives.test.tsx`:

- Button renders default `type="button"`, supports `size="lg"`, keeps disabled behavior.
- Form controls render control-only with existing API and wrapped label/helper/error with additive props.
- Error controls set `aria-invalid` and helper/error IDs.
- StatusBadge renders the correct visible text for all tone variants and existing shift statuses.
- KpiCard renders label/value/detail/icon.
- ProgressSteps exposes ordered step labels and current/completed states.
- EmptyState and Toast render accessible text and actions.

E2E scenarios, recommended after unit gates:

- Smoke `/`, `/caregiver/today`, `/coordinator/billing`, `/team`, `/clients` at desktop and mobile widths.
- Verify dark background/surface/text contrast and no clipped button/control text.
- Confirm billing flow behavior is unchanged with `tests/e2e/billing-flow.spec.ts`.

Stress/automated considerations:

- Test long labels, long helper/error text, disabled controls, all status tones, and narrow mobile width.
- Use Playwright screenshots only as supporting evidence; required green gates remain lint/typecheck/test/build.

## 5. Risks, Security, And Edge Cases

- Main risk is broad visual blast radius because token names are global.
- Hardcoded `bg-white`, `text-slate-*`, or `border-slate-*` outside shared primitives may remain visually inconsistent; patch only if they directly break the dark experience.
- `SelectHTMLAttributes` already has native `size`; use `controlSize` for visual sizing.
- This is frontend-only, so HIPAA/multi-tenancy/auth risk is low; do not touch tenant data flows or billing behavior.
- Preserve color-plus-word statuses for accessibility and avoid color-only state communication.
- Ensure contrast is acceptable for PHI-heavy operational screens.

## 6. Rollback And Verification

Rollback:

- Revert only the files listed in this plan.
- Do not reset or stash unrelated dirty worktree changes.
- No Convex codegen is required because this is frontend-only.

Verification sequence:

1. `npm run lint`
2. `npm run typecheck`
3. `npm run test`
4. `npm run build`

If any gate is red or unrun, mark the session `NOT COMPLETE / NOT GREEN` and include the failing command/output summary in the pipeline summary.

## 3. Implementation Approach

1. Update `src/index.css`.
   - Keep Tailwind token names like `bg-atria-bg`, `text-atria-ink`, `border-atria-border`.
   - Use alias variables so dark/light can swap later, for example `--color-atria-bg: var(--atria-bg)`.
   - Set dark defaults from the required brief: `#0A0E0F`, `#11171A`, `#0C1113`, `#F2F6F7`, `#16A34A`.
   - Add status tokens: success `#2FBF71`, warning `#F0B429`, danger `#F0564A`, info `#4D8DF6`, neutral `#8A99A0`.
   - Add Figma-adjacent supporting tokens: elevated/subtle surfaces, border `#2A3437`, secondary text, per-step accents, radius `8/12/16/20/pill`, control heights `40/52`, and type scale.
   - Move current light values into `[data-theme='light']` with a clear comment.

2. Polish existing primitives.
   - `Button`: add `size="lg"` as 52px, make primary/danger/secondary dark recipes, use pill radius for large primary, preserve `sm`, `md`, `icon`, `sidebar`.
   - `Card`: dark surface, border, radius 16, default content padding 24, with existing `className` overrides still winning.
   - `Input`, `Select`, `Textarea`: use 16px control text, md 40px and lg 52px recipes, dark focus ring, disabled state, additive label/helper/error support.
   - `StatusBadge`: support `success`, `warning`, `danger`, `info`, `neutral`; render visible word plus dot, never color alone; keep current `status: ShiftStatus` API.
   - `KpiCard`: dark KPI card recipe around the Figma 260x132 shape, preserving `label`, `value`, `detail`, `icon`.
   - Update `Badge`, `Checkbox`, `Dialog`, `Table`, `Separator`, and `AppLoader` to use the same dark tokens.

3. Add missing primitives.
   - `FieldGroup`: shared label/helper/error wrapper with `htmlFor`, `aria-describedby`, `aria-invalid` support.
   - `EmptyState`: icon/title/detail/actions presentational primitive.
   - `ProgressSteps`: accessible ordered step chips with per-step accent support.
   - `Toast`: presentational alert/toast row with tone, title, description, optional action/dismiss; no global toast manager.

4. Export everything from `src/shared/ui/index.ts`.

5. Keep scope presentation-only.
   - Do not alter route guards, data fetching, Convex calls, billing rules, or tenant logic.
   - Do not reset/stash/revert existing dirty worktree changes.

## 4. Test Plan

Unit tests in `src/shared/ui/uiPrimitives.test.tsx`:

- Button renders default `type="button"`, supports `size="lg"`, keeps disabled behavior.
- Form controls render control-only with existing API and wrapped label/helper/error with additive props.
- Error controls set `aria-invalid` and helper/error IDs.
- StatusBadge renders the correct visible text for all tone variants and existing shift statuses.
- KpiCard renders label/value/detail/icon.
- ProgressSteps exposes ordered step labels and current/completed states.
- EmptyState and Toast render accessible text and actions.

E2E scenarios, recommended after unit gates:

- Smoke `/`, `/caregiver/today`, `/coordinator/billing`, `/team`, `/clients` at desktop and mobile widths.
- Verify dark background/surface/text contrast and no clipped button/control text.
- Confirm billing flow behavior is unchanged with `tests/e2e/billing-flow.spec.ts`.

Stress/automated considerations:

- Test long labels, long helper/error text, disabled controls, all status tones, and narrow mobile width.
- Use Playwright screenshots only as supporting evidence; required green gates remain lint/typecheck/test/build.

## 5. Risks, Security, And Edge Cases

- Main risk is broad visual blast radius because token names are global.
- Hardcoded `bg-white`, `text-slate-*`, or `border-slate-*` outside shared primitives may remain visually inconsistent; patch only if they directly break the dark experience.
- `SelectHTMLAttributes` already has native `size`; use `controlSize` for visual sizing.
- This is frontend-only, so HIPAA/multi-tenancy/auth risk is low; do not touch tenant data flows or billing behavior.
- Preserve color-plus-word statuses for accessibility and avoid color-only state communication.
- Ensure contrast is acceptable for PHI-heavy operational screens.

## 6. Rollback And Verification

Rollback:

- Revert only the files listed in this plan.
- Do not reset or stash unrelated dirty worktree changes.
- No Convex codegen is required because this is frontend-only.

Verification sequence:

1. `npm run lint`
2. `npm run typecheck`
3. `npm run test`
4. `npm run build`

If any gate is red or unrun, mark the session `NOT COMPLETE / NOT GREEN` and include the failing command/output summary in the pipeline summary.
